# port/faces_sync.txt -- the FACE LEDGER for the main -> port sync.
#
# WHAT A FACE IS AND WHY A LEDGER. The decomp's ROM names are Itanium C names
# (_ZN13BigBrickBlock4KillEv) and the callers that reach them are extern "C", so
# the call is __cdecl with the receiver as the first stack argument. The bodies
# the synced tree compiles are real C++ members, __thiscall, receiver in ECX.
# An /alternatename is a NAME bridge and never an ABI bridge, so a row here
# cannot be closed with one: the alias would link, the receiver would be
# garbage, and nothing would say so (hal/method_faces.cpp failure class 3).
# What closes it is a FACE: a one-line extern "C" forwarder whose body is the
# qualified call ((Cls *)self)->Cls::meth(args).
#
# EVERY ROW IS DERIVED, NEVER GUESSED, and the derivation is re-run at build
# time by port/tools/facegen.py --sync. The three rules are in that file's
# "THE ADDRESS BINDING" block; in short:
#   1. the flat name is a ROM name at a known address in config/**/symbols.txt;
#   2. exactly ONE ROM address joins the decorated definition, so the face
#      cannot land on a plausible sibling (the ApproachLinear/ApproachLinear2
#      class, and the D1/D2 destructor pair that makes it bite here);
#   3. undname's class, method, parameter count and constness equal the ROM
#      name's, and the mangle's access/virtualness letter agrees with it.
# A row that stops deriving FAILS THE BUILD rather than silently leaving the
# link: a face that disappears is a receiver that goes back to garbage.
#
# THE RETURN TYPE IS DELIBERATELY NOT ONE OF THE THREE, and reading that as
# "the return type does not matter here" is what shipped a wrong answer at 191
# call sites. It is not part of the BINDING because MSVC decorates the return
# type, so a decorated name that joins at all already agrees about it. It IS
# part of the FACE: a bool member is returned in AL alone, and a reverse face
# that forwards the raw EAX hands three bytes of junk to every caller that
# spells the flat name int -- which the link cannot see, because an extern "C"
# symbol carries no return type. facegen now WIDENS a reverse face over
# bool/char/short to int with an explicit conversion, refuses to emit one that
# is not widened, and prints the whole widened set on every run. See THE RETURN
# WIDTH RULE in port/tools/facegen.py. Forward rows are excluded there and must
# stay excluded: they declare a flat callee they do not define.
#
# THE TOOL WILL NOT GENERATE A NAME THAT IS NOT IN THIS FILE. The ledger is the
# whole request in --sync mode, so a face reaches the binary only by being a
# reviewed, checked-in row here first.
#
# COLUMNS:  <ROM flat name>  <the decorated member>  <ROM address>  <R|F|D ...>
#
# The last column says which of the two names the FACE DEFINES:
#   R  reverse: the face defines the flat ROM name the caller asks for and
#      calls the member, ((Cls *)self)->Cls::meth(args).
#   F  forward: a caller TU declared its own shadow class and emitted a
#      __thiscall reference to a member of it; the face DEFINES that member
#      and calls the ROM body under its flat C name, receiver first. This is
#      hal/actor_class_faces.cpp's shape, derived instead of hand-written.
#   D  the DELETING DESTRUCTOR, and it carries two more columns:
#         <flat D0>  <the D1 it runs>  <ROM address>  D  <deallocation>  <heap>
#      The face defines the flat _ZN<Cls>D0Ev the link asks for, runs the
#      class's destructor (column two: the flat D1 when this link defines it,
#      else the MSVC destructor) and then makes the ONE call the ROM's own D0
#      body makes beyond its D1. The heap column is the symbol that body's
#      pooled literal relocates to, and "-" for the operator_delete2 shape,
#      which takes no heap. Several ROM names can sit on that pooled word, so
#      the column carries the spelling this link RESOLVES, never one still on
#      the wall. See the D0 RULE block in port/tools/facegen.py.
#   D  the DELETING DESTRUCTOR, and it carries two more columns:
#         <flat D0>  <the D1 it runs>  <ROM address>  D  <deallocation>  <heap>
#      The face defines the flat _ZN<Cls>D0Ev the link asks for, runs the
#      class's destructor (column two: the flat D1 when this link defines it,
#      else the MSVC destructor) and then makes the ONE call the ROM's own D0
#      body makes beyond its D1. The heap column is the symbol that body's
#      pooled literal relocates to, and "-" for the operator_delete2 shape,
#      which takes no heap. See the D0 RULE block in port/tools/facegen.py.
#
# WHAT IS NOT HERE, and why, so nobody adds it back by hand:
#   * a D0 whose ROM body is NOT its D1 plus exactly one deallocation. The D
#     rows below are the ones lane FACES2 proved are, by diffing each D0's
#     instruction stream against its D1's with the relocations resolved; the
#     ones that are not are listed with the diff in
#     runs/link100/out/FACES2/d0_refused.txt. A D0 still cannot be BOUND to a
#     definition -- MSVC's is ??_GCls@@UAEPAXI@Z, which C++ source cannot
#     name, and ??1 is the non-deleting sibling -- so a D row is written, not
#     bound, and what makes it honest is the ROM proof rather than a mangle.
#   * a D0 whose class has no destructor body in this link at all. 130 of the
#     356 are that; they need their D1 seated first and are listed in
#     runs/link100/out/FACES2/d0_needs_d1.txt.
#   * any class whose D1 and D2 (or C1 and C2) are two ROM addresses AND whose
#     two bodies are not the same body. Lane FACES3 added the STRUCTOR TWIN
#     RULE for the rest: the Itanium ABI emits D1 (complete) and D2 (base) as
#     two symbols and MSVC emits one destructor, so when the ROM proves the two
#     bodies identical the pair binds to that one definition, and when it does
#     not the row still refuses. The proof is in facegen.py's STRUCTOR TWIN
#     RULE block; the pairs it accepted and the one it refused (dCapEnemy_c,
#     whose D1 is in arm9 and whose D2 is in ov002) are in
#     runs/link100/out/FACES3/twin_proof.txt.
#   * float and double signatures, by-value structs, member pointers,
#     templates: refused by the emitter, listed with the reason in
#     runs/link100/out/FACES1/refused.txt.

# substitution, so the members have their real ROM bodies back.
#
# WHAT THE PAIR DID. Each of these rows defined the decorated member and called
# the flat ROM name; the flat ROM name is defined by a HAND FACE which calls the
# decorated member:
#     port/hal/actor_classes_ov081.cpp:879   _ZN8daGmch_c13InitResourcesEv

# so the two called each other with no body at the bottom. Moneybag (actor 261,

# call on its spawn path. out/SEATS2/bugs.md section 1 is the measurement.
#

# every one.
#

# RETIRED AT THE FOLD, run link100 wave 9b, lane INT3, 2026-09-14.
#
# The 3 rows below were F faces: the face DEFINED the decorated member and
# called the flat ROM name. The flat ROM name is defined by a hand face in
# port/hal/, whose body is the qualified call to that same decorated member.
# The two called each other and there was no body at the bottom, because the
# main -> port sync had quarantined src/actors/daPkn_c.cpp and taken the real
# bodies out of the link. Nothing in the tree compared the two directions and
# the link has not completed since, so no binary ever ran the pair. Lane
# SEATS2 found it: out/SEATS2/bugs.md section 1, and out/SEATS2/
# faces_after_seat.txt Part 2 is the retirement list.
#
# The seat is in the tree now (lane HOSTGEN4, port/slice_hostgen4.txt), so the
# member has its real ROM body back and the hand face calls a body. Retiring
# the row and deleting the matching hostgen LEDGER_PARK bracket are ONE
# change: retiring alone leaves the decorated name undefined, and unparking
# alone is a duplicate symbol (COMDAT selection 1, pick no duplicates).
#

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# THE FORWARD FACES THE STRUCTOR GATE USED TO REFUSE, and the two the
# batch-3 seats made derivable (lane FACES2). ??0Cls@@ and ??1Cls@@ carry
# no method-name component, so the forward direction read every one of
# them as "not a class-member mangle"; the gate was short, not the rule.
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# NOT ROWS: a forward face cannot DEFINE a structor. MSVC emits the
# class vftable ??_7Cls@@6B@ and its deleting destructor ??_GCls@@ from
# the same object, and references the vector one, operator delete and
# type_info besides -- measured, eight definitions and six references on
# one build surface. This file must not own any of them, and ??_G in
# particular is the name the whole D-row mechanism exists because nothing
# can spell. facegen refuses these with that reason now.
#__ZN7fBase_cC2Ev                                                         ??0fBase_c@@QAE@XZ                                               0x02043dec F
#__ZN7TornadoD1Ev                                                         ??1Tornado@@UAE@XZ                                               0x02136db0 F
#__ZN15daObjWc_Obj03_cD1Ev                                                ??1daObjWc_Obj03_c@@UAE@XZ                                       0x02111728 F
#__ZN9daSanbo_cD1Ev                                                       ??1daSanbo_c@@UAE@XZ                                             0x02135700 F
# NOT A ROW: the FACE CYCLE lane FACES1 found and guarded. A reverse row
# above already defines __ZN19BowserPuzzleManager8BehaviorEv and calls
# this member, so a forward row here would make the two call each other
# until the stack runs out. The guard refuses it; the ledger should not

# ---- BATCH 2: the STRUCTOR TWIN rows --------------------------------------
# A D1/D2 or C1/C2 pair is not an overload. The Itanium ABI emits one body
# twice and MSVC emits it once, so the pair binds to that one definition when
# the ROM proves the two bodies are the same body: one module, equal sizes,
# equal body-relative relocations, and identical bytes once the relocated words
# are masked out. Twenty-seven of the twenty-eight pairs the wall asks for are
# byte-identical; dCapEnemy_c is not (its D1 is in arm9 and its D2 in ov002)
# and ModelAnim has two MSVC destructors, so those three rows stay refused.

__ZN5FaderD1Ev                                                           ??1Fader@@UAE@XZ                                                 0x0201786c R

# ---- BATCH 3: nested type names and scalar references ---------------------
# A parameter or return type that names a NESTED class (Particle::System,
# dPa_c::level_c::callback_c::Entry) is an ordinary pointer at the ABI, but it
# cannot be forward-declared from outside its enclosing class, so the shadows
# are now written OUT OF LINE in an order that declares every name before it is
# used. A reference to a SCALAR (short &, signed char &) is a pointer too, and
# the face re-lands it as *(T *)a. A CLASS-spelled parameter stays refused:
# MSVC mangles a class as V where this file's struct shadows mangle U, and the
# one row of that shape here (Particle::SysTracker::Contents::Create) is in
# runs/link100/out/FACES3/refused.txt.

# ---- BATCH 4: the twelve D0 rows lane FACES2 held back ---------------------
# FACES2 proved 343 of the sync wall's 356 D0 bodies are "the D1 plus exactly
# one deallocation" and refused thirteen whose instruction diff said more than
# that. Its note says the build-time relocation check would accept twelve of
# them, that they differ from their D1 in REGISTER ALLOCATION rather than in
# relocations, and that a lane which wants them should normalise the receiver
# register and re-run the diff rather than weaken it. The diff was re-run:
# both bodies disassembled out of the ROM image with each literal pool skipped,
# every pooled load resolved through config/**/relocs.txt and every branch
# resolved to its target. Twelve are their D1 plus the deallocation once two
# things are accounted for -- the receiver register a former leaf has to move
# into r4 because it now makes a call, and the branch displacements that shift
# with the body's address. The full streams are in
# runs/link100/out/FACES3/d0_evidence.txt.
#
# _ZN15dScMgSnowball_cD0Ev is NOT here. It swaps two independent pairs of
# instructions against its D1, which is almost certainly the scheduler moving
# around the extra tail, but it is not one contiguous insertion and this lane
# had no ruling that says how far a reorder may go.
__ZN12daBgSnmBdy_cD0Ev                                                   _ZN12daBgSnmBdy_cD1Ev                                            0x0211f048 D _ZN6Memory10DeallocateEPvP4Heap GAME_HEAP_PTR

__ZN4HeapD0Ev                                                            _ZN4HeapD1Ev                                                     0x0203ca20 D _ZN6Memory16operator_delete2EPv -
__ZN4dBgWD0Ev                                                            _ZN4dBgWD1Ev                                                     0x02039668 D _ZN6Memory16operator_delete2EPv -
__ZN5FaderD0Ev                                                           _ZN5FaderD1Ev                                                    0x02017848 D _ZN6Memory16operator_delete2EPv -

# ---- BATCH 5: the overloads the join key could not tell apart --------------
# Rule 2's key is (class, method) and drops the parameter list, so the three
# ROM overloads of DetectClsn on each of dBgW, dBgW_KcMbg and dBgW_KcMbgSclY
# landed on one key and every one of them refused as a plausible sibling. They
# are not siblings in the dangerous sense: each ROM name spells its own
# parameter types, each MSVC definition spells the same types its own way, and
# the two spellings join. The rule refuses on every kind of doubt -- ROM names
# in one join whose parameter CLASSES are not pairwise distinct (two overloads
# differing only in a scalar type) are still refused, and so is a join where
# more or fewer than one MSVC definition matches.

# does not touch a single row below.

# non-deleting destructor. main's cleanup wave gave these classes real headers
# that DECLARE the destructor, so MSVC stops synthesising one and emits
# ??1X@@UAE@XZ out of the class's own TU. The decorated half of that pair left
# walk_window's wall inside the sync merge itself; what these rows close is the
# other half, the flat ROM D1 name the port's actor tables call, which nothing
# bound. The two members are the same story one level down.
#

# facegen --sync re-derives all 44 at build time, so a row that stops deriving
# fails the build rather than quietly leaving the link.

#
# out/HALROWS/settled.txt section A is the join, done the only way that is
# evidence: config/**/symbols.txt gives the flat name's ROM address,
# config delinks.txt gives the TU that owns that address, and THAT TU'S OWN
# OBJECT was dumped to read the decorated spelling it emits. Fourteen of the
# fifteen ambiguous rows turned out to be ONE ROM BODY PLUS ONE PORT-SIDE
# SHADOW: a host file in port/hal or port/unmatched declares a private shadow of
# the class to reach one member and spells it with a different return type or a
# different virtualness, MSVC mangles the two apart, and facegen read one
# function as two overloads and refused to bind either.
#
# EIGHT OF THOSE SHADOWS ARE RETIRED IN THIS COMMIT, each with the reason and
# the measurement in the file that carried it: hal/heap_vtable.cpp (three),
# hal/lk4_solidheap_seat.cpp, hal/reverse_bridges.cpp (the global-::State
# spelling only) and unmatched/Mg3DEsp_Faces.cpp (two). Every one was measured
# first with dumpbin over all 8662 of walk_window's link inputs and had ZERO
# referencing objects, so retiring it adds no wall row. FIVE MORE STAY, because
# a real caller TU spells the member that way and a host bridge for such a
# caller is legitimate; those are recorded in facegen.py's
# PORT_SHADOW_DEFINITIONS table, which drops a shadow from a candidate list only
# when the definition it shadows is in that same list.

__ZN13SharedFilePtr8LoadFileEv                                           ?LoadFile@SharedFilePtr@@QAEPAXXZ                                0x02017bc4 R

# block is target_sources(walk_window ...) alone while the ledger reaches all

# reversing this retirement and dropping the forwarder instead, closes it.

# The fifteenth ambiguous row is the ONE GENUINE OVERLOAD PAIR on this wall: a
# real no-argument MemoryNode::Target constructor beside the one-argument one.
# ARITY alone picks it, and facegen could not count the Itanium side because the
# parameter is a back-reference (PS_). It resolves now: subst_table spells out
# what S_ names, which for _ZN10MemoryNode6TargetC1EPS_ is MemoryNode (the
# prefix, not the class itself), and the definition the ROM address names spells
# the same type PAU1@.
__ZN10MemoryNode6TargetC1EPS_                                            ??0Target@MemoryNode@@QAE@PAU1@@Z                                0x0204e938 R

# The two rows out/HALROWS/settled.txt section C had to refuse an /alternatename
# because both definitions are __thiscall and an alias is a name bridge and
# never an ABI bridge. Their bindings were settled there and only the bridge was
# missing; what kept facegen from writing it was the NAME on the Itanium side in
# both cases, not the binding. dBgPi::operator= mangles its method as the code
# aS (ITANIUM_OPERATORS) and std::type_info as the abbreviation St, and the
# destructor pair needed the same abbreviation honoured when the twin rule
# re-spells the stem.
__ZN5dBgPiaSERKS_                                                        ??4dBgPi@@QAEAAU0@ABU0@@Z                                        0x02038018 R
__ZNSt9type_infoD1Ev                                                     ??1type_info@std@@UAE@XZ                                         0x020736e4 R

#    word: lane DTORS2 struck Tornado out of its forwarder file because
#    emitting that class's inline destructor emits its vftable too, and the
#    vftable references every virtual slot, of which two exist in this link
#    only under the ROM's flat names. Same cause as Toad and daTree_c above,
#    same answer. Addresses from config/arm9/overlays/ov096/symbols.txt:45,46;
#    both flat bodies are defined in this link (neither src TU carries a
#    "recovered from vtable slot identity" marker) and neither decorated name
#    is defined by anything else, so neither row can duplicate. These two close
#    no wall row of their own -- nothing references the decorated spellings
#    today -- they make Tornado D0 and D1 forwardable.

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# THE THREE ACTOR DESTRUCTOR PAIRS THE SEATS DID NOT SUPPLY (wave-9c fold,
# lane INT4).
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------
# out/DTORS2/handoff.txt part 2 left daMip_c D0/D1, daMky_c D0 and daGmch_c D0
# open on a stated test: once the seat lands, does the seated object define
# ??1<Cls>@@UAE@XZ. The test was run in this tree with dumpbin over all three
# seated objects and the answer is NO -- none of them defines a destructor, a
# vftable or a flat structor name -- so dtorfwd's out-of-line premise cannot
# reach them and adding them to BATCHES[5] would only make LNK2001 rows.
#
# BUT THE DECORATED DESTRUCTOR IS IN THE LINK ANYWAY, which is the part the
# handoff's test could not see, because it looked at the seated object alone.
# ??1daGmch_c@@UAE@XZ is a COMDAT emitted by port/hal/dtor_faces_cpp.cpp, whose
# CPPD1 macro odr-uses the class's inline destructor, and ??1daMip_c@@UAE@XZ and
# ??1daMky_c@@UAE@XZ are defined elsewhere in the same link. All three are in
# the 49804-name defined-external universe of walk_window's own link inputs.
# So each class is an ordinary R row and not a forwarder question at all.
#
# THE D0 HALF IS A D ROW AND NOT AN R ROW, and facegen refuses the R shape for
# it in so many words: D0 is the DELETING destructor, MSVC's is the scalar
# deleting destructor ??_G<Cls>@@UAEPAXI@Z which C++ source cannot name, and
# binding D0 to ??1 would drop the free. The D row is the mechanism that
# exists for exactly this: the flat D0 calls the flat D1 and then
# Memory::Deallocate, which is what the cartridge's D0 does.
#
# The daMky_c and daGmch_c D1 rows close no wall row of their own -- nothing
# asks for those two flat names today -- they are what the D row beside each
# one calls. Addresses derived by facegen against config, not typed by hand:
# daMip_c 0x0212a6d4 / 0x0212a724, daMky_c 0x02111688 / 0x021116d0, daGmch_c
# 0x02126504 / 0x02126554.
__ZN7daMip_cD1Ev                                                         ??1daMip_c@@UAE@XZ                                               0x0212a6d4 R
__ZN7daMky_cD1Ev                                                         ??1daMky_c@@UAE@XZ                                               0x02111688 R
__ZN8daGmch_cD1Ev                                                        ??1daGmch_c@@UAE@XZ                                              0x02126504 R

__ZN7daMip_cD0Ev                                                         _ZN7daMip_cD1Ev                                                  0x0212a724 D _ZN6Memory10DeallocateEPvP4Heap data_020a0eac
__ZN7daMky_cD0Ev                                                         _ZN7daMky_cD1Ev                                                  0x021116d0 D _ZN6Memory10DeallocateEPvP4Heap data_020a0eac
__ZN8daGmch_cD0Ev                                                        _ZN8daGmch_cD1Ev                                                 0x02126554 D _ZN6Memory10DeallocateEPvP4Heap data_020a0eac
